J_{\alpha^{\prime}}^{(u,u^{\prime})}(X)=L^{(u)}X
if
u^{\prime}=-
and
J_{\alpha^{\prime}}^{(u,u^{\prime})}(X)=XL^{(u)}
if
u^{\prime}=+
. Finally,
\sigma_{\omega}^{(u,u^{\prime})}(s^{\prime})=\omega^{-2}\textnormal{Tr}_{\mathcal{A}}(\sigma_{\alpha}^{(\bar{u})}\sigma_{\alpha}\rho_{\omega,\delta}(s^{\prime}/\omega^{2}))
if
\alpha=\alpha^{\prime}
and
\sigma_{\omega}^{(u,u^{\prime})}(s^{\prime})=u^{\prime}\omega^{-2}\textnormal{Tr}_{\mathcal{A}}(\sigma_{\alpha^{\prime}}^{(\bar{u})}\sigma_{\alpha}\rho_{\omega,\delta}(s^{\prime}/\omega^{2}))
if
\alpha\neq\alpha^{\prime}
. We note that for any
u,u^{\prime}
,
{|\hskip-1.084pt|\mathcal{K}_{\alpha}|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{\diamond},{|\hskip-1.084pt|\mathcal{J_{\alpha^{\prime}}^{(u,u^{\prime})}}|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{\diamond}\leq 2
and, using Lemma
9
,
{|\hskip-1.084pt|\sigma_{\omega}^{(u,u^{\prime})}(s^{\prime})|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{1}\leq 1/4+\omega^{-1}\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}\delta/2+\omega^{-2}\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}\delta
. Thus, applying Eq.
144
and Lemma
14
(c), we obtain that

\sum_{\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}{\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s/\omega^{2}}\textnormal{Tr}(O(t-s)\mathcal{Q}_{\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{(4)}(s^{\prime}))e^{-2(s/\omega^{2}-s^{\prime})}ds^{\prime}ds\right|}\leq\left(2+4\frac{\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}\delta}{\omega}+8\frac{\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}\delta}{\omega^{2}}\right)\zeta^{(3)}(\gamma).

(148)

Considering Eqs.
145
-
148
, we may choose
\lambda^{(2)}(\gamma)=2\zeta^{(3)}(\gamma)
to satisfy the lemma statement. Note that
\zeta^{(3)}(\gamma)
depends on
a
and
\mathcal{Z}
and that by Lemma
14
, so long as
k({|S_{O}|},\gamma)\leq O\left(\exp(\gamma^{-\kappa})\right)
as
\gamma\to 0
, then
\zeta^{(3)}(\gamma)\leq O\left(\gamma^{-(2d+1)(\kappa+1)}\right)
as
\gamma\to 0
.

Next, using the definition of
\mathcal{K}^{(0)}_{\beta}(t)
from Lemma
8
and the fact that
O(t-s)
is not supported on the ancillae, we have that
\textnormal{Tr}(O(t-s)\mathcal{K}_{\beta}^{(0)}\left(s/\omega^{2}\right))=\textnormal{Tr}(O(t-s)\mathcal{N}_{\beta}(\sigma_{\omega}(s)))
where
\sigma_{\omega}(s)=\rho_{\omega,\delta}(s/\omega^{2})
. Noting that
{|\hskip-1.084pt|\mathcal{N}_{\beta}|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{\diamond}\leq 2
and that
{|\hskip-1.084pt|\sigma_{\omega}(s)|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{1}\leq 1
, we apply Lemma
14
(b) to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\beta}{\left|\int_{0}^{t}\textnormal{Tr}(O(t-s)\mathcal{K}_{\beta}^{(0)}\left(s/\omega^{2}\right))ds\right|}\leq\zeta^{(2)}(\gamma),

(149)

where in the application of Lemma
14
we consider
d(S_{O},S^{\prime}_{\beta})=d(S_{O},\tilde{S}^{\prime}_{\beta})
. Picking
\lambda^{\prime(1)}(\gamma)=\zeta^{(2)}(\gamma)
satisfies the lemma statement. Note that
\zeta^{(2)}(\gamma)
depends on
\mathcal{Z}
,
\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}
, and
a
.

In order to prove the last statement in this lemma, we must define a way to sum over all regions
S_{\alpha}
and
S^{\prime}_{\beta}
in a single summation. For
c\in\{1,2,...,M+M^{\prime}\}
, we define
S^{\prime\prime}_{c}=S_{c}
if
c\leq M
and
S^{\prime\prime}_{c}=S^{\prime}_{c-M}
if
c>M
. Remember that
M
is the number of target Lindbladian terms
\mathcal{L}_{\alpha}
and that
M^{\prime}
is the number of error Lindbladian terms
\mathcal{N}_{\beta}
. Given any
c
, we define the number other regions
S^{\prime\prime}_{c^{\prime}}
that intersect
S^{\prime\prime}_{c}
to be bounded by some
\mathcal{Z}^{\prime\prime}\geq 0
, i.e.
{|\{c^{\prime}|S^{\prime\prime}_{c^{\prime}}\cap S^{\prime\prime}_{c}\neq\emptyset\}|}\leq\mathcal{Z}^{\prime\prime}
. If
c>M
, then
S^{\prime\prime}_{c}=S^{\prime}_{c-M}
can intersect a maximum of
\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}
other regions
S^{\prime\prime}_{c^{\prime}}
. If
c\leq M
, then
S^{\prime\prime}_{c}=S_{c}
can intersect a maximum of
\mathcal{Z}
other regions
S_{\alpha}
and each of the
{|S_{c}|}
points in
S_{c}
can be in the support of a maximum of
\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}
regions
S^{\prime\prime}_{\beta}
. We can bound
\max_{\alpha}{|S_{\alpha}|}
by some
V_{S}
and note that
V_{S}
is a function of only
a
and
d
. Hence we can choose
\mathcal{Z}^{\prime\prime}=\max(\mathcal{Z^{\prime}},\mathcal{Z}+V_{S}\mathcal{Z}^{\prime})
.

Using these definitions, from Lemma
8
, we have that
\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\textnormal{Tr}(O(t-s)\mathcal{K}^{(1)}_{\alpha,\beta}(s^{\prime}/\omega^{2}))=\sum_{c,c^{\prime}}\textnormal{Tr}(O(t-s)\mathcal{K}_{c}\mathcal{J}_{c^{\prime}}(\sigma_{\omega}(s^{\prime})))+\textnormal{h.c.}
where
\mathcal{K}_{\alpha}(X)=-i[L_{c}^{\dagger},\sigma_{c}X]
if
c\leq M
and
0
otherwise,
\mathcal{J}_{c^{\prime}}=\mathcal{N}_{c^{\prime}}
if
c^{\prime}>M
and
0
otherwise, and
\sigma_{\omega}(s^{\prime})=\rho_{\omega,\delta}(s^{\prime}/\omega^{2})
. We note that
{|\hskip-1.084pt|\mathcal{K}_{c}|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{\diamond},{|\hskip-1.084pt|\mathcal{J}_{c^{\prime}}|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{\diamond}\leq 2
and
{|\hskip-1.084pt|\sigma_{\omega}(s^{\prime})|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{1}\leq 1
. Thus, applying Eq.
144
and Lemma
14
(c), we obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha,\beta}{\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s/\omega^{2}}e^{-2(s/\omega^{2}-s^{\prime})}\textnormal{Tr}\big(O(t-s)\mathcal{K}_{\alpha,\beta}^{(1)}(s^{\prime})\big)ds^{\prime}ds\right|}\leq 2{\zeta^{\prime}}^{(3)}(\gamma).

(150)

Using the definition of
\mathcal{K}^{(2)}_{\alpha,\beta}
in Lemma
8
, we have that
\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\textnormal{Tr}(O(t-s)K^{(2)}_{\alpha,\beta}(s^{\prime}/\omega^{2}))=\sum_{c,c^{\prime}}\textnormal{Tr}(O(t-s)\mathcal{K}_{c}\mathcal{J}_{c^{\prime}}(\sigma_{\omega}(s^{\prime})))+\textnormal{h.c.}

where
\mathcal{K}_{c}(X)=[L_{c}^{\dagger},L_{c}X]
if
c\leq M
and
0
otherwise,
\mathcal{J}_{c^{\prime}}=\mathcal{N}_{c^{\prime}}
if
c^{\prime}>M
and
0
otherwise, and
\sigma_{\omega}(s^{\prime})=\rho_{\omega,\delta}(s^{\prime}/\omega^{2})/2
. We note that
{|\hskip-1.084pt|\mathcal{K}_{c}|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{\diamond},{|\hskip-1.084pt|\mathcal{J}_{c^{\prime}}|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{\diamond}\leq 2
and
{|\hskip-1.084pt|\sigma_{\omega}(s^{\prime})|\hskip-1.084pt|}_{1}\leq 1/2
. Thus, applying Eq.
144
and Lemma
14
(c), we obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha,\beta}{\left|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s/\omega^{2}}e^{-2(s/\omega^{2}-s^{\prime})}\textnormal{Tr}\big(O(t-s)\mathcal{K}_{\alpha,\beta}^{(2)}(s^{\prime})\big)ds^{\prime}ds\right|}\leq{\zeta^{\prime}}^{(3)}(\gamma).

(151)

By Lemma
14
,
\zeta^{(3)}(\gamma)\leq O\left(\gamma^{-(2d+1)(\kappa+1)}\right)
as
\gamma\to 0
and
\zeta^{(3)}(\gamma)
depends on
\mathcal{Z}
,
a
, and
\mathcal{Z}^{\prime\prime}
. Picking
\lambda^{\prime(2)}(\gamma)=2\zeta^{(3)}(\gamma)
satisfies the lemma statement.
∎
